# Review: study_sweep_scalefree_20261020.mjs vs SWEEP_RECLAIM_SCALEFREE_PREREG_20261020.md

VERDICT: H1 machinery (walk-forward rank, top band, reclaim, symmetric race, day-blocked CI, 6 criteria, placebo) is implemented as pre-registered; NOT SUPPORTED is the correct verdict the code produces. Real deviations/weaknesses below; none would flip the null, but two are definitional (ATR-normalized rank vs prereg "ATR only for placebo"; race barrier grows with depth so deep events rarely resolve).

VERIFIED (file:line):
(1) Walk-forward rank — CLEAN, consistent, not lookahead. script:30 computes rank/top from F.depths.length BEFORE script:31 pushes the current depth; sess is chronological (script:10-11 over loader:22 sorted dates). Every event's depth = level - min(signed low) over [i0, min(reclaim, windowEnd)] (script:21), same rule for all. Subtlety (not a bug): a NON-reclaiming prior's depth runs to window end (systematically deeper), so the p80 threshold is inflated by these "survivor" depths and reclaiming events are less likely to clear top — conservative.

(2) top vs placebo p80 — EQUIVALENT. Real: script:30 top = depth >= sorted[floor(n*0.8)] over n prior depths. Placebo: script:50 uses F.p80[m-1], where F.p80 is pushed at script:31 as p80 of the first m depths, and m = real events before date == n (one event per family per session). Same threshold. Two caveats: (a) "rank>=.8" is coded as depth >= floor(n*0.8) value => rank >= (floor(0.8n)+1)/n (~.817 at n=60), slightly stricter than the doc's "rank >= 0.8"; the descriptive [.8,1] band (script:40, e.rank>=.8) therefore differs slightly from H1's top set. (b) placebo depth is ATR-normalized too, so placebo ranking is consistent with real.

(3) Race scoring — no sign/off-by-one error. script:23: E=C[tr] (reclaim close), barriers E+q*dpts and E-q*dpts, loop j=tr+1..<A.to, order 'b'/'w'/'l'; sgn=-1 mirror correct (upHit=L<=E-q*dpts->'w', dnHit=H>=E+q*dpts->'l'); both and neither score 0 (script:34). Two notes: (a) bar tr's own range is skipped (race starts tr+1), so the reversal thrust on the reclaim bar itself is never counted — conservative, can hide the initial impulse. (b) dpts is raw points (script:22), so the barrier is q*(raw depth), matching the prereg's "own depth" in points.

(4) Globex geometry — mostly correct, one deviation. script:15 GLX {from:n0,to:a,lastStart:null,PDL:[pdl,pdh,0]}; script:19 first=n0+30 (18:30); script:20 has NO GLX cutoff (break is RTH-only), so penetration runs to 09:29, not the doc's 09:25 (doc:11 "18:30..09:25"); reclaim/race to a=09:30 (doc "ends 09:30" OK). Prior session: script:11 pd=S.dates[j-1] => Monday uses Friday, Tuesday uses Monday — correct; weekend window n0=addDay(d,-1)+'18:00' (script:12) = Sun 18:00 for Monday — correct. Caveats: S.dates holds only FULL RTH days (loader:22 b-a>300), so after a half-day pd skips it; the gap>4 test skips long-weekend Mondays (conservative).

(5) Placebo null — fair-ish, as pre-registered. script:50 shifts the low level by ±[0.25,1.0]ATR, same detect, reclaim, ranked against the REAL family p80 (F.p80[m-1]); matches doc:15. Caveats (inferred): real levels are exact extremes, so ~half the shifted levels sit above the real low (easier sweep) and half below — a mixed null; a level shifted <1 ATR can still lie inside the same overnight/prior-day range, weakening the null. RNG is one shared LCG (script:7) across CI/placebo/Spearman — deterministic but not independently seeded.

(6) What could hide a real effect:
  - Race scaled by own depth (script:23): deeper sweep => larger q*dpts => fewer resolutions before window end => 'n' dominates => S pulled to 0. Structural power limit, not a bug.
  - Only reclaiming events enter H1 (script:47): deep sweeps that never reclaim are invisible.
  - Reclaim bar's own range excluded (see (3)) drops the reversal's first bar.
  - No "by level family" breakdown printed (prereg doc:20 lists it) — script:39-41 aggregates ONL/PDL/IBL.
  - Depth used for RANK is ATR-normalized (script:22) but prereg doc:11 says ATR20 is "used only for the placebo shift size"; the race uses raw points. Inconsistent with prereg, makes the rank regime-dependent.

WHAT THE TEST COULD NOT SHOW: it cannot separate "no edge after a deep reclaim" from "edge exists but the within-session, own-depth-scaled race rarely resolves and the reclaim bar's first move is excluded". It says nothing about deep sweeps that do NOT reclaim, nothing per level family, and (given ATR normalization) the top band is a vol-adjusted, not raw-points, depth band. Globex n=30 is descriptive only.

Suggested changes: (i) enforce the GLX 09:25 penetration cutoff; (ii) rank on raw dpts, or amend the prereg to state ATR normalization; (iii) make top literally rank>=0.8; (iv) print the per-level-family band table; (v) use a barrier that doesn't scale with depth (or fixed ATR points) and/or start the race at bar tr; (vi) independent RNG seeds.

Review complete. I read the script, the prereg doc, and the loader it depends on (`pocJoinLoader.mjs`) to verify bar-indexing, session boundaries, ATR20, and walk-forward ordering. The final answer is written to the top of `/home/mmoniz/trading-journal/scratch/deepseek_response.md` (25 lines, under 80).

Summary of findings:

**Verified clean (no bug):**
- **(1) Walk-forward rank** — rank/top computed from `F.depths.length` *before* the current depth is pushed (script:30–31), over chronological sessions. Depth definition is consistent across events and has no lookahead.
- **(2) `top` flag vs placebo p80** — equivalent; both reduce to "depth ≥ p80 of the prior `n==m` depths" of the same family.
- **(3) Race scoring** — no sign or off-by-one error; the `sgn=-1` mirror is correct.
- **(4) Globex prior-session / weekend geometry** — Monday→Friday, Sunday 18:00 open both correct.

**Deviations / weaknesses flagged:**
- GLX penetration cutoff: doc says `09:25`, code runs to `09:29` (no `lastStart` for GLX).
- Depth used for **ranking** is ATR-normalized (script:22), contradicting prereg "ATR20 used only for the placebo shift size"; the race barrier uses raw points.
- `rank>=0.8` is implemented slightly stricter (~`0.817`), and the descriptive `[.8,1]` band differs from H1's `top` set.
- "By level family" breakdown (prereg line 20) is never printed.
- Race starts at `tr+1`, so the reclaim bar's own reversal thrust is excluded.

**What the test could not show:** it can't separate "no edge" from "edge exists but the own-depth-scaled race rarely resolves within session and the reclaim bar's move is dropped"; it's blind to deep sweeps that never reclaim, blind per level family, and the "top" band is vol-adjusted rather than raw-depth.
